## W3 - PR-tier CI Cost on develop

Local workflow file: `.github/workflows/test.yml`
```yaml
name: Tests

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main
      - develop

concurrency:
  group: test-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # Simplified matrix: 5 configurations
        # - Ubuntu: test across Python versions (min, middle, latest stable)
        # - macOS/Windows: single version for compatibility testing
        # Note: Python 3.9 dropped (EOL Oct 2025), 3.14 deferred (lxml wheel missing)
        os: [ubuntu-latest]
        python-version: ['3.10', '3.12', '3.13']
        include:
          - os: macos-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
          - os: windows-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
          - os: windows-latest
            python-version: '3.10'

    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Create virtual environment
        run: uv venv

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run linting
        run: |
          uv run ruff check .
          uv run ruff format --check .
          uv run -- python scripts/check_windows_antipatterns.py

      - name: Run type checking
        run: |
          uv run ty check

      - name: Run fast tests
        run: |
          # -p pytest_cov re-enables coverage plugin (disabled by default in pytest.ini)
          uv run -- python -m pytest tests -m "fast and not (slow or stress or resource_heavy or live_integration)" --tb=short -p pytest_cov --cov=benchbox --cov-report=xml --cov-report=term-missing --cov-fail-under=70

      - name: Upload coverage to Codecov
        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest' && matrix.python-version == '3.12'
        uses: codecov/codecov-action@v4
        with:
          file: ./coverage.xml
          fail_ci_if_error: false  # Don't fail CI if Codecov upload fails (token may not be configured)
          token: ${{ secrets.CODECOV_TOKEN }}

  # Check for unsafe patch() string paths in test files.
  # Catches the pattern patch("benchbox.cli.commands.<module>.<attr>") where
  # <module> is re-exported by __init__.py under the same name, shadowing the
  # submodule and causing Python 3.10 failures or order-dependent 3.12 passes.
  check-test-patterns:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Run patch safety check
        # Bare python is intentional: this job skips uv setup for speed since
        # check_patch_safety.py is stdlib-only (no third-party imports).
        run: python scripts/check_patch_safety.py

  # Security audit
  security:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    env:
      # Comma-separated GHSA/CVE IDs for temporarily accepted false positives.
      # Keep empty unless triage is documented in the TODO item.
      PIP_AUDIT_IGNORE_VULNS: ""
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run security audit
        run: |
          if [ -n "${PIP_AUDIT_IGNORE_VULNS}" ]; then
            IGNORE_ARGS=$(printf '%s' "${PIP_AUDIT_IGNORE_VULNS}" | tr ',' '\n' | sed '/^$/d;s/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//;/^$/d;s/^/--ignore-vuln /' | tr '\n' ' ')
            uvx pip-audit ${IGNORE_ARGS}
          else
            uvx pip-audit
          fi

  # Quick integration smoke tests - runs on ALL PRs
  integration-smoke:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - os: ubuntu-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
          - os: windows-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run integration smoke tests
        run: |
          # Run platform smoke tests and fast integration tests (~46 tests)
          # These don't require external credentials and catch common issues
          uv run -- python -m pytest tests/integration -m "platform_smoke or (integration and fast)" --tb=short

  # Required lane for optional table-format dependencies.
  # Without this, delta/iceberg integration assertions can be silently skipped.
  integration-table-formats:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run table-format integration tests
        run: |
          uv run -- python -m pytest -q tests/integration/core/data_organization/test_presorted_generation.py -m "requires_table_formats" --tb=short

  # Full integration test suite - runs on push to main or with label
  integration:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    if: github.event_name == 'push' || (github.event_name == 'pull_request' && contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'run-integration'))
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: |
          uv run -- python -m pytest tests/integration -m "integration and not slow and not stress" --tb=short --maxfail=5 --timeout=300

  # Package build and install test
  test-package:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - os: ubuntu-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
          - os: windows-latest
            python-version: '3.12'
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Build package
        run: |
          uv build

      - name: Test package installation
        shell: bash
        run: |
          # Use bash on all platforms so glob expansion works cross-platform.
          # GitHub Actions Windows runners have Git Bash available.
          uv venv test-venv
          uv pip install --python test-venv dist/*.whl
          uv run --python test-venv python -c "import benchbox; print('Package installation successful')"
          uv run --python test-venv benchbox --help

      - name: Upload build artifacts
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: test-build-dist-${{ matrix.os }}
          path: dist/
          retention-days: 7  # Shorter retention for test artifacts

  # CLI↔explorer visualization parity check.
  # Verifies that the checked-in JSON fixtures in tests/parity/fixtures/
  # match the current Python implementations, AND that the Vitest suite passes.
  # Skipped on Release v* PRs (curation + version bumps don't change fixtures).
  parity:
    if: ${{ !startsWith(github.event.pull_request.title, 'Release v') }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Set up Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '20'

      - name: Install Python dependencies
        run: uv sync --group dev

      - name: Install Node dependencies
        run: npm ci
        working-directory: results-explorer

      - name: Verify parity fixtures match Python source
        run: make parity-check

      - name: Run Vitest parity suite
        run: npm test -- --reporter=verbose
        working-directory: results-explorer

  # PySpark tests - requires Java, runs separately
  # Non-blocking: PySpark setup can be fragile
  pyspark-tests:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    continue-on-error: true
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - name: Set up Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Install uv
        uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          uv sync --group dev

      - name: Run PySpark tests
        run: |
          make test-pyspark
```

## Conclusion

Executive summary: the original strict-base diagnosis is stale. `develop` has no classic branch protection, and the active `develop` ruleset already has strict required status checks disabled with only `lint` and `test (ubuntu-latest, 3.12)` required. The heavy `Tests` workflow still runs broadly on PRs and branch pushes, so CI-cost simplification remains useful, but downstream work must not spend effort disabling strict-base or reducing required checks that are already reduced.

Step 1: GO. Opt-in local pre-push, bounded fanout, and conservative test-lock cleanup are still useful local-loop changes independent of strict-base.

Step 2: GO. A retained worktree pool with claim/release remains useful for predictable parallel work and is independent of branch-protection state.

Step 3: RE-PLAN before implementation. The strict-base toggle and required-check reduction are already true for `develop`; keep only the parts that reduce unnecessary workflow execution cost, such as moving heavy non-required lanes out of PR-tier or adding path-aware skips.

Step 3a: GO after Step 3 re-plan. Path-based CI skip still has value for non-code PRs because `.github/workflows/test.yml` runs many jobs on every pull request to `develop`, but the implementation must preserve the already-minimal required contexts.

Step 4: RE-PLAN before implementation. `develop-post-merge.yml` is currently healthy, so auto-revert behavior should be conservative and explicitly designed around false-positive and cancellation handling.

Step 5: GO. Measurement is still valuable, but the current W5 baseline is 0.0% strict-base proxy rate, so queue-related thresholds should start from a presumption that no queue is needed.

Step 6: NO-GO unless later Step 5 metrics contradict this record. Current evidence shows no strict-base re-CI pain in the recent merged PR sample, and native GitHub Merge Queue is not exposed for this user-owned repository.

Must-preserve check: no branch protection, ruleset, or workflow file was modified while producing this record.
